SELECT r.guid, r."CompanyId", r."Designation", r."FirstName", r."IsDeleted", r."LastName", r."PhoneNumber", r."UserId", s.guid, s."Description", s."EmploymentPosition", s."EmploymentType", s."IsDeleted", s."LastDate", s."Maxsalary", s."Minsalary", s."PostedDate", s."RecruiterID", s."Title", s.guid0, s."AppliedOn", s."IsDeleted0", s."JobPostId", s."SeekerId", s."Status"
FROM recruiters AS r
LEFT JOIN (
    SELECT j.guid, j."Description", j."EmploymentPosition", j."EmploymentType", j."IsDeleted", j."LastDate", j."Maxsalary", j."Minsalary", j."PostedDate", j."RecruiterID", j."Title", j0.guid AS guid0, j0."AppliedOn", j0."IsDeleted" AS "IsDeleted0", j0."JobPostId", j0."SeekerId", j0."Status"
    FROM "jobPosts" AS j
    LEFT JOIN "jobApplications" AS j0 ON j.guid = j0."JobPostId"
) AS s ON r.guid = s."RecruiterID"
WHERE NOT (r."IsDeleted")
ORDER BY r.guid, s.guid
